\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Conditional Granger Causality Analysis: token_entropy vs ECE → diversity metrics.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nimport json, os, sys, warnings\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport pandas as pd\\\\nfrom statsmodels.tsa.api import VAR\\\\nfrom itertools import product\\\\n\\\\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\\\\\\\"ignore\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\nBASE = \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nEXPERIMENTS = {\\\\n    \\\\\\\"a050_s42\\\\\\\":   f\\\\\\\"{BASE}/alpha_sweep/a050_s42/all_metrics.json\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"a050_s43\\\\\\\":   f\\\\\\\"{BASE}/alpha_sweep/a050_s43/all_metrics.json\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"a050_s44\\\\\\\":   f\\\\\\\"{BASE}/alpha_sweep/a050_s44/all_metrics.json\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"a055_s42\\\\\\\":   f\\\\\\\"{BASE}/alpha_sweep/a055_s42_fp32/all_metrics.json\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"a075_s42\\\\\\\":   f\\\\\\\"{BASE}/alpha_sweep/a075_s42/all_metrics.json\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"a075_s43\\\\\\\":   f\\\\\\\"{BASE}/alpha_sweep/a075_s43/all_metrics.json\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"a075_s44\\\\\\\":   f\\\\\\\"{BASE}/alpha_sweep/a075_s44/all_metrics.json\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"pythia410m\\\\\\\":  f\\\\\\\"{BASE}/generalize/pythia410m_wiki_a100_s42/all_metrics.json\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"opt1b\\\\\\\":       f\\\\\\\"{BASE}/generalize/opt1b_wiki_a100_s42/all_metrics.json\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"pythia1b_c4\\\\\\\": f\\\\\\\"{BASE}/generalize/pythia1b_c4_a100_s42/all_metrics.json\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"smollm3_3b\\\\\\\":  f\\\\\\\"{BASE}/generalize/smollm3_3b_wiki_a100_s42_fp32_v3/all_metrics.json\\\\\\\",\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nCANARIES = [\\\\\\\"token_entropy\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"ece\\\\\\\"]\\\\nDOWNSTREAMS = [\\\\\\\"distinct_1\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"distinct_2\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"distinct_3\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"mauve\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_experiment(path):\\\\n    with open(path) as f:\\\\n        data = json.load(f)\\\\n    df = pd.DataFrame(data).sort_values(\\\\\\\"generation\\\\\\\").reset_index(drop=True)\\\\n    return df\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef conditional_granger(df, cause, effect, condition, maxlag=2):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Conditional Granger: does `cause` Granger-cause `effect` after controlling for `condition`?\\\\n\\\\n    Compare:\\\\n    - Unrestricted: VAR on [effect, cause, condition]\\\\n    - Restricted:   VAR on [effect, condition] (cause excluded)\\\\n\\\\n    Uses statsmodels VAR.test_causality which does an F-test.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    cols_needed = [cause, effect, condition]\\\\n    if any(c not in df.columns for c in cols_needed):\\\\n        return None\\\\n\\\\n    sub = df[cols_needed].dropna()\\\\n    T = len(sub)\\\\n\\\\n    # Need enough observations for VAR estimation\\\\n    # VAR(p) needs T > p*k + k where k = number of variables\\\\n    results = {}\\\\n    for lag in range(1, maxlag + 1):\\\\n        k = 3  # unrestricted model variables\\\\n        min_obs = lag * k + k + 1\\\\n        if T < min_obs:\\\\n            results[lag] = {\\\\\\\"p_value\\\\\\\": np.nan, \\\\\\\"F_stat\\\\\\\": np.nan, \\\\\\\"df\\\\\\\": (np.nan, np.nan),\\\\n                           \\\\\\\"note\\\\\\\": f\\\\\\\"T={T} too small for lag={lag}\\\\\\\"}\\\\n            continue\\\\n        try:\\\\n            model = VAR(sub[[effect, cause, condition]])\\\\n            fitted = model.fit(lag)\\\\n            test = fitted.test_causality(caused=effect, causing=cause, kind='f')\\\\n            results[lag] = {\\\\n                \\\\\\\"p_value\\\\\\\": test.pvalue,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"F_stat\\\\\\\": test.test_statistic,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"df\\\\\\\": (test.df_num, test.df_denom),\\\\n            }\\\\n        except Exception as e:\\\\n            results[lag] = {\\\\\\\"p_value\\\\\\\": np.nan, \\\\\\\"F_stat\\\\\\\": np.nan, \\\\\\\"note\\\\\\\": str(e)[:80]}\\\\n\\\\n    return results\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef unconditional_granger(df, cause, effect, maxlag=2):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Unconditional (bivariate) Granger: cause → effect.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    cols = [cause, effect]\\\\n    if any(c not in df.columns for c in cols):\\\\n        return None\\\\n    sub = df[cols].dropna()\\\\n    T = len(sub)\\\\n    results = {}\\\\n    for lag in range(1, maxlag + 1):\\\\n        k = 2\\\\n        min_obs = lag * k + k + 1\\\\n        if T < min_obs:\\\\n            results[lag] = {\\\\\\\"p_value\\\\\\\": np.nan, \\\\\\\"F_stat\\\\\\\": np.nan,\\\\n                           \\\\\\\"note\\\\\\\": f\\\\\\\"T={T} too small for lag={lag}\\\\\\\"}\\\\n            continue\\\\n        try:\\\\n            model = VAR(sub[[effect, cause]])\\\\n            fitted = model.fit(lag)\\\\n            test = fitted.test_causality(caused=effect, causing=cause, kind='f')\\\\n            results[lag] = {\\\\n                \\\\\\\"p_value\\\\\\\": test.pvalue,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"F_stat\\\\\\\": ───\\nprint(\\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\"*80)\\nprint(\\\"EFFECT SIZE: Partial R² from VAR(1)\\\")\\nprint(\\\"=\\\"*80)\\n\\nfor exp_name, path in EXPERIMENTS.items():\\n    if not os.path.exists(path):\\n        continue\\n    df = load_experiment(path)\\n    T = len(df)\\n    if T < 7:\\n        continue\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n  {exp_name} (T={T}):\\\")\\n    for downstream in DOWNSTREAMS:\\n        if downstream not in df.columns:\\n            continue\\n\\n        cols_full = [downstream, \\\"token_entropy\\\", \\\"ece\\\"]\\n        cols_no_ent = [downstream, \\\"ece\\\"]\\n        cols_no_ece = [downstream, \\\"token_entropy\\\"]\\n\\n        try:\\n            sub = df[cols_full].dropna()\\n            if len(sub) < 7:\\n                continue\\n\\n            # Full model R²\\n            m_full = VAR(sub).fit(1)\\n            resid_full = m_full.resid[downstream]\\n            ss_full = (resid_full**2).sum()\\n\\n            # Without entropy\\n            m_no_ent = VAR(df[cols_no_ent].dropna()).fit(1)\\n            resid_no_ent = m_no_ent.resid[downstream]\\n            ss_no_ent = (resid_no_ent**2).sum()\\n\\n            # Without ECE\\n            m_no_ece = VAR(df[cols_no_ece].dropna()).fit(1)\\n            resid_no_ece = m_no_ece.resid[downstream]\\n            ss_no_ece = (resid_no_ece**2).sum()\\n\\n            # Partial R²\\n            partial_r2_ent = 1.0 - ss_full / ss_no_ent if ss_no_ent > 0 else np.nan\\n            partial_r2_ece = 1.0 - ss_full / ss_no_ece if ss_no_ece > 0 else np.nan\\n\\n            print(f\\\"    {downstream:<12}: partial_R²(entropy)={partial_r2_ent:+.4f}, partial_R²(ECE)={partial_r2_ece:+.4f}\\\")\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            print(f\\\"    {downstream:<12}: ERROR {str(e)[:60]}\\\")\\n\\n\\n# ─── Save full results as JSON ───\\noutput = {\\n    \\\"meta\\\": {\\n        \\\"analysis\\\": \\\"Conditional Granger Causality\\\",\\n        \\\"canaries\\\": CANARIES,\\n        \\\"downstreams\\\": DOWNSTREAMS,\\n        \\\"method\\\": \\\"VAR F-test via statsmodels\\\",\\n    },\\n    \\\"results\\\": {}\\n}\\n\\nfor exp_name, exp_data in all_results.items():\\n    exp_out = {\\\"T\\\": exp_data[\\\"T\\\"], \\\"pairs\\\": {}}\\n    for downstream, tests in exp_data[\\\"tests\\\"].items():\\n        pair_out = {}\\n        for test_key, lag_results in tests.items():\\n            if lag_results is None:\\n                continue\\n            pair_out[test_key] = {}\\n            for lag, r in lag_results.items():\\n                pair_out[test_key][str(lag)] = {\\n                    k: (float(v) if isinstance(v, (int, float, np.floating)) else\\n                        [float(x) for x in v] if isinstance(v, tuple) else v)\\n                    for k, v in r.items()\\n                }\\n        exp_out[\\\"pairs\\\"][downstream] = pair_out\\n    output[\\\"results\\\"][exp_name] = exp_out\\n\\nout_path = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/analysis/conditional_granger_v2.json\\\"\\nos.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out_path), exist_ok=True)\\nwith open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n    json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\\nprint(f\\\"\\\\n\\\\nResults saved to {out_path}\\\")\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.51}}"}}}

conditional_granger(df, cause, effect, condition, maxlag=2):\n    cols_needed = [cause, effect, condition]\n    if any(c not in df.columns for c in cols_needed):\n        return None\n\n    sub = df[cols_needed].dropna()\n    T = len(sub)\n\n    results = {}\n    for lag in range(1, maxlag + 1):\n        k = 3\n        min_obs = lag * k + k + 1\n        if T < min_obs:\n            results[lag] = {\"p_value\": np.nan, \"F_stat\": np.nan, \"df\": (np.nan, np.nan),\n                           \"note\": f\"T={T} too small for lag={lag}\"}\n            continue\n        try:\n if any(c not in df.columns for c in cols):\n        return None\n    sub = df[cols].dropna()\n    T = len(sub)\n    results = {}\n    for lag in range(1, maxlag + 1):\n        k = 2\n        min_obs = continue\n        try:\n            model = VAR(sub[[effect, cause]])\n            fitted = model.fit(lag)\n            test = fitted.test_causality(caused=effect, causing=cause, kind='f')\n Exception as e:\n            results[lag] = {\"p_value\": np.nan, \"F_stat\": np.nan, \"note\": str(e)[:80]}\n    return results\n\n\ndef joint_granger(df, causes, effect, maxlag=2):\n    cols = [effect] + causes\n    if any(c not in df.columns for c in cols):\n        return None\n    sub = df[cols].dropna()\n    T = len(sub)\n    results = {}\n    for lag in range(1, maxlag + 1):\n        k = len(cols)\n        min_obs = lag * k + k + 1\n        if T < min_obs:\n            results[lag] = {\"p_value\": np.nan, \"F_stat\": np.nan,\n                           \"note\": f\"T={T} too small for lag={lag}\"}\n            continue\n        try:\n            model = VAR(sub)\n            fitted = model.fit(lag)\n            test = fitted.test_causality(caused=effect, causing=causes, kind='f')\n            results[lag] = {\n                \"p_value\": test.pvalue,\n                \"F_stat\": test.test_statistic,\n                \"df\": (test.df_num, test.df_denom),\n   tests[\"uncond_entropy\"] = unconditional_granger(df, \"token_entropy\", downstream)\n        tests[\"uncond_ece\"] = unconditional_granger(df, \"ece\", downstream)\n        tests[\"cond_entropy_given_ece\"] = conditional_granger(df, \"token_entropy\", downstream, \"ece\")\n        tests[\"cond_ece_given_entropy\"] = conditional_granger(df, \"ece\", downstream, \"token_entropy\")\n        tests[\"joint\"] = joint_granger(df, [\"token_entropy\", \"ece\"], downstream)\n\n        exp_results[\"tests\"][downstream] = tests\n\n        for test_name, lag_results in tests.items():\n            if lag_results is None:\n                continue\n            for lag, r in lag_results.items():\n                p = r.get(\"p_value\", np.nan)\n                sig = \"***\" if p < 0.01 else \"**\" if p < 0.05 else \"*\" if p < 0.10 else \"\"\n                if not np.isnan(p):\n                    print(f\"    {downstream:<12} | {test_name:<25} | lag={lag} |           \"redundant\": 0, \"neither\": 0, \"na\": 0}\n\nfor exp_name, exp_data in all_results.items():\n    for downstream, tests in exp_data[\"tests\"].items():\n        def get_p(key):\n            if key not in tests or tests[key] is None:\n                return np.nan\n            return tests[key].get(1, {}).get(\"p_value\", np.nan)\n\n        p_ue = get_p(\"uncond_entropy\")\n        p_uc patterns05[\"na\"] += 1\n            continue\n\n        ent_indep = p_ce < 0.05\n        ece_indep = p_cc < 0.05\n\n        if ent_indep and ece_indep:\n            patterns05[\"both_independent\"] print(f\"  {pattern:<20}: {count}\")\n\n# Same at α=0.10\npatterns10 = {\"entropy_only\": 0, \"ece_only\": 0, \"both_independent\": 0,\n              \"redundant\": 0, \"neither\": 0, \"na\": patterns10[\"redundant\"] += 1\n        else:\n            patterns10[\"neither\"] += 1\n\nprint(\"\\nAt α=0.10:\")\nfor pattern, count in patterns10.items():\n    print(f\"  {pattern:<20}: EXPERIMENTS.items():\n    if not os.path.exists(path):\n        continue\n    df = load_experiment(path)\n    if \"token_entropy\" in df.columns and \"ece\" in df.columns:\n        r = df[\"token_entropy\"].corr(df[\"ece\"])\n        print(f\"  {exp_name:<20}: r = {r:+.4f}\")\n\n\n# ─── Effect size (partial R²) ───\nprint(\"\\n\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)\nprint(\"EFFECT SIZE: Partial R² from VAR(1)\")\nprint(\"=\" * 80)\n\nfor exp_name, path in EXPERIMENTS.items():\n    if not os.path.exists(path):\n        continue\n    df = load_experiment(path)\n    T = len(df)\n    if T < 7:\n        continue\n\n    print(f\"\\n  {exp_name} (T={T}):\")\n    for downstream in DOWNSTREAMS:\n        if downstream not in df.columns:\n            continue\n\n        cols_full = [downstream, \"token_entropy\", \"ece\"]\n        cols_no_ent = [downstream, \"ece\"]\n        cols_no_ece = [downstream, \"token_entropy\"]\n\n        try:\n            sub = df[cols_full].dropna()\n            if len(sub) < 7:\n                continue\n\n            m_full = VAR(sub).fit(1)\n            resid_full = m_full.resid[downstream]\n            ss_full = (resid_full**2).sum()\n\n            m_no_ent = VAR(df[cols_no_ent].dropna()).fit(1)\n            resid_no_ent = m_no_ent.resid[downstream]\n            ss_no_ent = (resid_no_ent**2).sum()\n\n            m_no_ece = VAR(df[cols_no_ece].dropna()).fit(1)\n            resid_no_ece = m_no_ece.resid[downstream]\n            ss_no_ece = (resid_no_ece**2).sum()\n\n            partial_r2_ent = 1.0 - ss_full / ss_no_ent {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016LiQ8o39W3bjWbw3LdrfwR","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DRLVrUP3g32uie6wed1JwF","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-19828","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 -c \"\nimport json, numpy as np\nd = json.load(open('/root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/analysis/conditional_granger_v2.json'))\nprint('OLD n_experiments:', len(d['results']))\nprint('OLD experiments:', list(d['results'].keys()))\n\npatterns05 = {'entropy_only': 0, 'ece_only': 0, 'both_independent': 0, 'redundant': 0, 'neither': 0, 'na': 0}\npatterns10 = {'entropy_only': 0, 'ece_only': 0, 'both_independent': 0, 'redundant': 0, 'neither': 0, 'na': 0}\ntotal = 0\nfor exp_name, exp_data in d['results'].items():\n    for ds, tests in exp_data['pairs'].items():\n        total += 1\n        def get_p(key):\n            if key not in tests: return float('nan')\n            return tests[key].get('1', {}).get('p_value', float('nan'))\n        p_ue = get_p('uncond_entropy')\n        p_uc = get_p('uncond_ece')\n        p_ce = get_p('cond_entropy_given_ece')\n        p_cc = get_p('cond_ece_given_entropy')\n        for alpha, pat in [(0.05, patterns05), (0.10, patterns10)]:\n            if np.isnan(p_ce) or np.isnan(p_cc):\n                pat['na'] += 1; continue\n            ent = p_ce < alpha; ece = p_cc < alpha\n            if ent and ece: pat['both_independent'] += 1\n            elif ent and not ece: pat['entropy_only'] += 1\n            elif not ent and ece: pat['ece_only'] += 1\n            elif p_ue < alpha or p_uc < alpha: pat['redundant'] += 1\n            else: pat['neither'] += 1\nprint(f'Total pairs: {total}')\nprint('OLD Dominance alpha=0.05:', patterns05)\nprint('OLD Dominance alpha=0.10:', patterns10)\n\n# pR2 comparison for shared experiments\nfor exp in ['a050_s42', 'a075_s42', 'smollm3_3b']:\n    if exp in d['results']:\n        print(f'\\\\nOLD {exp} T={d[\\\"results\\\"][exp][\\\"T\\\"]}')\n\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1555,"cache_read_input_tokens":81428,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1555},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3a3574ba-3b45-489f-b006-cf1e8aa906cb","uuid":"b52a495b-894c-49b7-a073-496faaa8500b"}
